{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01NXKCvQeVJHN9JAhB9Edc9J","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_012pA37P4DRL98JzDry1Qqpw","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/environment/skills/pddl-fundamentals.md","content":"# PDDL Fundamentals\n\n## Overview\nPDDL (Planning Domain Definition Language) is a standardized language for expressing classical planning problems. It separates domain knowledge from problem instances.\n\n## Core Components\n\n### Domain File Structure\nA PDDL domain defines:\n- **Types**: Categories of objects (airplane, segment, direction)\n- **Predicates**: Boolean state properties (at-segment, occupied, blocked)\n- **Constants**: Fixed objects of specific types\n- **Actions**: Operators that change state through preconditions and effects\n\n### Problem File Structure\nA PDDL problem specifies:\n- **Objects**: Instances of types defined in the domain\n- **Initial state**: Set of facts that are true at the start\n- **Goal**: Desired state to achieve\n\n## Key Concepts\n\n### State Representation\n- A state is a set of ground facts (predicates with concrete objects)\n- Facts can be asserted as true or explicitly marked as false\n- Closed World Assumption: facts not mentioned are false\n\n### Actions\n```\n(:action action_name\n  :parameters (?param1 - type1 ?param2 - type2)\n  :precondition (and (fact1 ?param1) (fact2 ?param2))\n  :effect (and (new_fact ?param1) (not (old_fact ?param2)))\n)\n```\n\n- **Parameters**: Variables used in the action\n- **Precondition**: Conditions that must be true to execute\n- **Effect**: What becomes true/false after execution\n\n## Planning Problem Solving Strategy\n\n1. **Parse the domain and problem**\n   - Identify available actions\n   - Understand initial state and goal\n\n2. **Search for a plan**\n   - Use forward chaining: apply actions from initial state\n   - Use backward chaining: work backward from goal\n   - Check action applicability using preconditions\n\n3. **Validate the plan**\n   - Ensure each action's preconditions are met\n   - Verify final state satisfies goal\n   - Check all objects are properly typed\n\n## Ground Facts vs Variables\n- **Variables** (in domain): Start with `?` (e.g., `?a`)\n- **Ground facts** (in problem/plan): Use actual object names (e.g., `at-segment airplane_CFBEG seg_rw_0_400`)\n- **Action instantiation**: Replace variables with objects from the problem\n\n## Common Predicates in Airport Domain\n- `(at-segment ?a - airplane ?s - segment)`: Airplane is at segment\n- `(occupied ?s - segment)`: Segment has a plane\n- `(blocked ?s - segment ?a - airplane)`: Segment blocked by plane\n- `(is-moving ?a - airplane)`: Plane is in motion\n- `(is-parked ?a - airplane ?s - segment)`: Plane is parked\n- `(facing ?a - airplane ?d - direction)`: Plane's direction\n\n## Planning Algorithm Tips\n\n### Forward Search (State-Space)\n- Start from initial state\n- Repeatedly apply applicable actions\n- Stop when goal state reached\n- Simple but can have large search space\n\n### Backward Search (Regression)\n- Start from goal\n- Find actions that achieve goal facts\n- Build chain backward to initial state\n- More efficient for goal-oriented problems\n\n### Heuristics\n- **Relaxed plan heuristic**: Ignore negative effects\n- **Subgoal counting**: Number of unsatisfied goals\n- **Critical path**: Longest causal chain needed\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":140,"cache_read_input_tokens":24606,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":140,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd09e235-c623-4496-ac37-b7fb0c36ca01","uuid":"dace1d61-1303-47dc-af6f-168334cd313d"}

Domain name: `(define (domain NAME) ...)`\n2. Requirements: `:requirements`\n3. Types and constants: `:types` and `:constants`\n4. Predicates: `:predicates`\n5. Actions: `:action` sections with parameters, preconditions, effects\n\n### Step 2: Problem Parsing\nExtract from problem file:\n1. Problem name: `(define (problem NAME) ...)`\n2. Domain reference: `(:domain DOMAIN_NAME)`\n3. Objects: `:objects` (additional typed objects)\n4. Initial state: `:init` (set of true facts)\n5. Goal: `:goal` (desired state specification)\n\n## Key Parsing Patterns\n\n### Types and Objects\n```\n:types airplane segment direction airplanetype\n\n:constants\n    north south - direction\n    light medium heavy - airplanetype\n    airplane_CFBEG - airplane\n\n:objects\n    (additional objects in problem file)\n```\n\n### Predicates Declaration\n```\n:predicates\n    (at-segment ?a - airplane ?s - segment)\n    (occupied ?s - Regression\nFor each goal fact:\n- If it's added by action's effect: add preconditions\n- If it's removed by action's effect: remove it from goal\n- Keep other facts unchanged\n\n## State items = []\n        index += 1\n        while tokens[index] != ')':\n            item, index = parse_sexp(tokens, index)\n            items.append(item)\n        return items, index + 1\n    else:\n types\n    obj_combinations = product(\n        *[objects[ptype] for ptype in param_types]\n    )\n\n    ground_actions = []\n    for obj_combo in obj_combinations:\n        subst = Python PDDL Solver Implementation\n\n## Quick Start with Python\n\n### Libraries to Consider\n- **No external dependencies** for basic solver\n- **collections**: defaultdict, deque for search\n- **re**: Regular expressions for parsing\n- **typing**: Type hints for clarity\n\n### Basic Structure\n```python\nfrom typing import Set, Tuple, List, Dict, Optional\nfrom collections import deque\nimport re\n\nclass PDDLParser:\n    def parse_domain(self, filename: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Parse PDDL domain file\"\"\"\n        with open(filename) as f:\n            content = f.read()\n        return self._parse_pddl(content)\n\n    def parse_problem(self, filename: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Parse PDDL problem file\"\"\"\n        with open(filename) as f:\n            content = f.read()\n        return self._parse_pddl(content)\n\n    def _parse_pddl(self, content: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Generic PDDL parser\"\"\"\n        # Remove comments\n        content = re.sub(r';;.*', '', content)\n        # Tokenize and parse\n        tokens = self._tokenize(content)\n        return self._parse_tokens(tokens)\n\nclass State:\n    \"\"\"Represent a planning state\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, facts: Set[Tuple]):\n        self.facts = frozenset(facts)\n\n    def __hash__(self):\n        return hash(self.facts)\n\n    def __eq__(self, other):\n        return self.facts == other.facts\n\n    def __repr__(self):\n        return f\"State({len(self.facts)} facts)\"\n\nclass Action:\n    \"\"\"Represent a ground action instance\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, name: str, precond: Set[Tuple],\n                 positive_effects: Set[Tuple],\n                 negative_effects: Set[Tuple]):\n        self.name = name\n        self.precond = precond\n        self.positive_effects = positive_effects\n        self.negative_effects = negative_effects\n\n    def is_applicable(self, state: State) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if action can be applied to state\"\"\"\n        return self.precond <= state.facts\n\n    def apply(self, state: State) -> State:\n        \"\"\"Apply action and return new state\"\"\"\n        new_facts = (state.facts - self.negative_effects) | self.positive_effects\n        return State(new_facts)\n\n    def __repr__(self):\n        return self.name\n\nclass Planner:\n    \"\"\"Forward state-space planner\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, actions: List[Action], initial_state: State,\n                 goal: Set[Tuple]):\n        self.actions = actions\n        self.initial_state = initial_state\n        self.goal = goal\n\n    def search(self, max_depth: Optional[int] = None) -> Optional[List[Action]]:\n        \"\"\"BFS forward search\"\"\"\n        queue = deque([(self.initial_state, [])])\n        visited = {self.initial_state}\n        depth = 0\n\n        while queue:\n            if max_depth and depth > max_depth:\n                return None\n\n            state, plan = queue.popleft()\n            depth = max(depth, len(plan))\n\n            if self.goal <= state.facts:\n                return plan\n\n            for action in self.actions:\n                if action.is_applicable(state):\n                    next_state = action.apply(state)\n                    if next_state not in visited:\n                        visited.add(next_state)\n                        queue.append((next_state, plan + [action]))\n\n        return None\n\nclass PDDLSolver:\n    \"\"\"Complete PDDL problem solver\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, domain_file: str, problem_file: str):\n        parser = PDDLParser()\n        self.domain = parser.parse_domain(domain_file)\n        self.problem = parser.parse_problem(problem_file)\n\n    def solve(self) -> Optional[List[str]]:\n        \"\"\"Solve the planning problem\"\"\"\n        # Extract components from domain and problem\n        actions = self._instantiate_actions()\n        initial_state = self._build_initial_state()\n        goal = self._build_goal()\n\n        # Run search\n        planner = Planner(actions, initial_state, goal)\n        plan = planner.search()\n\n        if plan:\n            return [self._action_to_string(a) for a in plan]\n        return None\n\n    def _instantiate_actions(self) -> List[Action]:\n        \"\"\"Ground all parameterized actions\"\"\"\n        # TODO: Implement action grounding\n        pass\n\n    def _build_initial_state(self) -> State:\n        \"\"\"Convert initial state from problem\"\"\"\n        facts = set()\n        for init_fact in self.problem['init']:\n            facts.add(tuple(init_fact))\n        return State(facts)\n\n    def _build_goal(self) -> Set[Tuple]:\n        \"\"\"Convert goal specification\"\"\"\n        goal_facts = set()\n        for goal_fact in self.problem['goal']:\n            goal_facts.add(tuple(goal_fact))\n        return goal_facts\n\n    def _action_to_string(self, action: Action) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Convert action to PDDL format\"\"\"\n        return action.name\n```\n\n## Parsing Tips\n\n### Tokenizing PDDL\n```python\ndef tokenize(content: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Split PDDL into tokens\"\"\"\n    content = re.sub(r';;[^\\n]*', '', content)  # Remove comments\n    content = re.sub(r'[():]', lambda m: ' ' + m.group() + ' ', content)\n    return content.split()\n```\n\n### Building S-expressions\n```python\ndef parse_sexp(tokens: List[str], index: int) -> Tuple[any, int]:\n    \"\"\"Parse one S-expression starting at index\"\"\"\n    if tokens[index] == '(':\n        items = []\n        index += 1\n        while tokens[index] != ')':\n            item, index = parse_sexp(tokens, index)\n            items.append(item)\n        return items, index + 1\n    else:\n        return tokens[index], index + 1\n```\n\n## Action Instantiation\n\n```python\ndef instantiate_action(action_template: Dict, objects: Dict) -> List[Action]:\n    \"\"\"Create ground actions from parameterized template\"\"\"\n    param_names = [p[0] for p in action_template['parameters']]\n    param_types = [p[1] for p in action_template['parameters']]\n\n    # Get all objects of required types\n    obj_combinations = product(\n        *[objects[ptype] for ptype in param_types]\n    )\n\n    ground_actions = []\n    for obj_combo in obj_combinations:\n        subst = dict(zip(param_names, obj_combo))\n        precond = substitute_facts(action_template['precondition'], subst)\n        effects_pos = substitute_facts(action_template['effects_positive'], subst)\n        effects_neg = substitute_facts(action_template['effects_negative'], subst)\n\n        ground_actions.append(Action(\n            f\"{action_template['name']}({','.join(obj_combo)})\",\n            precond, effects_pos, effects_neg\n        ))\n\n    return ground_actions\n```\n\n## Handling Large Domains\n\n### Lazy Instantiation\n- Only ground actions when needed\n- Improves memory efficiency\n\n### State Compression\n- Use bitsets for large state representations\n- Hash facts for faster comparison\n\n### Iterative Deepening\n- Combine DFS space efficiency with BFS optimality\n- Set depth limits dynamically\n\n## Testing and Validation\n\n```python\ndef validate_plan(plan: List[Action], initial_state: State, goal: Set[Tuple]):\n    \"\"\"Verify plan is valid\"\"\"\n    current = initial_state\n\n    for action in plan:\n        assert action.is_applicable(current), f\"Action not applicable: {action}\"\n        current = action.apply(current)\n\n    assert goal <= current.facts, \"Goal not achieved\"\n    return True\n```\n\n## Performance Considerations\n\n- **State explosion**: Limit search depth, use heuristics\n- **Memory**: Use frozenset for hashable Airplane has correct type\n2. Airplane at source segment\n3. Airplane in correct state (is-moving)\n4. Airplane facing correct direction\n5. Destination segment unoccupied\n6. Destination segment not blocked by this airplane\n\n### Effects Implement\n1. Remove airplane from source segment\n2. Mark source as unoccupied\n3. Add airplane to destination segment\n4. Mark destination as occupied\n5. Update blocked predicates\n6. Maintain facing direction\n\n## Common Action Types\n\n### Movement Actions\n- **push_back**: Airplane pushed backward (tow truck assists)\n- **drive**: Airplane drives under own power\n- **land**: Airplane lands on runway\n- **takeoff**: Airplane departs from runway\n\n### State Change Actions\n- **start_engines**: Enable movement\n- **stop_engines**: Disable movement\n- **turn**: Change facing direction\n\n## Planning Challenges\n\n### Spatial Conflicts\n- Only one airplane per segment\n- Blocked areas prevent multiple planes from interfering\n- Careful ordering of moves required\n\n### Type Constraints\n- Aircraft type determines which paths available\n- Heavy aircraft restricted to wider taxiways\n- Light aircraft can use narrow parking positions\n\n### Turning Constraints\n- Airplanes can't move in wrong direction\n- Must turn at intersections\n- Turn maneuvers take actions\n\n### Resource Ordering\n- Park/gate availability\n- Runway availability (conflicting landings/takeoffs)\n- Tug availability for push-back\n\n## Solution Strategies\n\n### For Small Problems (task01-task02)\n1. **Direct path finding**: Identify target parking position\n2. **Backwards reasoning**: What must happen before parking?\n3. **Simple execution**: Chain moves to reach goal\n4. **Minimal turns**: Direct path if possible\n\n### For Larger Problems\n1. **Hierarchical planning**: Break into phases\n   - Takeoff phase: Move from parking to runway\n   - Landing phase: Move from runway to parking\n2. **Conflict resolution**: Handle multiple aircraft\n3. **Resource scheduling**: Manage shared resources\n\n### Search Strategy Recommendations\n- **BFS**: Guaranteed optimal solution for small instances\n- **Greedy best-first**: Faster but non-optimal\n- **A* with heuristic**: Good balance for medium sizes\n- **Depth-first with iterative deepening**: Space-efficient\n\n## Domain-Specific Optimizations\n\n### Relaxed Heuristics\n- Ignore type constraints\n- Assume all segments passable\n- Measure shortest path to goal location\n\n### Critical Path Analysis\n- Identify forced action sequences\n- Distinguish from optional detours\n- Helps with search guidance\n\n### State Abstraction\n- Group similar segments by region\n- Focus on macro-movements first\n- Expand to detailed paths later\n\n## Example Simple Plan\n```\nmove(airplane_CFBEG, seg_rw_0_400, seg_rwe_0_50, south, south, medium)\nmove(airplane_CFBEG, seg_rwe_0_50, seg_rwte1_0_10, south, south, medium)\nmove(airplane_CFBEG, seg_rwte1_0_10, seg_twe3_0_50, south, south, medium)\n...eventually reaches seg_pp_0_60\npark(airplane_CFBEG, seg_pp_0_60, south, medium)\n```\n\n## Debugging Tips\n\n### Validation\n1. self.index = 0\n\n    def parse_file(self, filename: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Parse a PDDL file and return structured data\"\"\"\n        with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n            content = f.read()\n\n        # Remove comments\n        content = re.sub(r';;[^\\n]*', '', content)\n\n        # Tokenize\n        self.tokens = self._tokenize(content)\n        self.index = 0\n\n        # Parse top-level structure\n        return self._parse_top_level()\n\n    def _tokenize(self, content: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Convert PDDL content to tokens\"\"\"\n        # Replace  \"\"\"Ground a single action template\"\"\"\n        params = action_template.get('parameters', [])\n\n        if not params:\n            # No parameters, create single ground action\n            action_name = action_template['name']\n            precond = self._extract_facts(action_template.get('precondition', []))\n            effects_pos, effects_neg =
